# MCP conformance matrix

Generated by `internal/conformance` (`MCPX_UPDATE_MATRIX=1 go test ./internal/conformance -run TestMatrix`); do not edit.

Generated from inputs `d0058ac9e67ce8b1` (sha256 of docs/spec/requirements.md, internal/conformance/overrides/*.json, internal/conformance/coverage*.go). `TestMatrix` regenerates this file and fails when it differs, so it describes the tree it is committed in.

Every normative requirement of every revision (catalogue: `docs/spec/requirements.md`), for mcpx as a server and as a client. A requirement is *tested* when a named test verifies it in that revision, *n/a* when it does not bind mcpx in that role, and a *gap* when it binds mcpx and mcpx does not meet it yet, with the issue that tracks it. Run exactly the covering tests with `go run ./internal/conformance/cmd/covering`.
